Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any galaxies module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 925. The projective resolution has length at most 431 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by galaxies generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 925, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 431. QED.
